- **strict-mode**: 「strict mode」「辛口で」「容赦なく正直に」「忖度なしで」「本音で」と言われたとき、 肯定や忖度を排し、容赦なく正直な高レベルアドバイザーとして応答するモードに切り替える。 思考の前提・盲点・機会費用を指摘し、優先順位付きの行動計画まで提示する。 ([kokuto821/matagi](https://github.com/kokuto821/matagi/tree/HEAD/plugins/matagi/skills/strict-mode) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **strict-mode-enforcer**: Triggers automatically during security-critical operations, sensitive terminal commands, data migration, or when zero-trust execution is requested. ([JackSmack1971/fullstack-council](https://github.com/JackSmack1971/fullstack-council/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/strict-mode-enforcer) · ★1)
- **strict-mode-migrator**: Plan and run a move to --!strict for a whole codebase, one module at a time: leaves first, cheapest first inside each layer, cycles flagged, progress counted. Use when a project has many untyped scripts; luau-type-annotator does each single module. ([romangigliotti123-arch/vault-kit-01](https://github.com/romangigliotti123-arch/vault-kit-01/tree/HEAD/claude-kit/skillpack/scripting/strict-mode-migrator) · 2 copies)
- **strict-module**: Провести модуль через зачистку под строгую типизацию - поставить `// @strict-types`, довести диагностики EDT до нуля, переписать докблоки, исправить найденные дефекты и закоммитить отдельным коммитом. Использовать когда владелец называет модуль для зачистки, просит включить строгую типизацию, убрать ошибки EDT в модуле. К модулям, заимствованным из БСП, не применять. ([vandalsvq/edt1c-ai-template](https://github.com/vandalsvq/edt1c-ai-template/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/strict-module) · ★23)
- **strict-policy**: Operationalization of AGENTS.md R1-R5 plus the RDD and ADE pillars — the engineering-discipline rules that come BEFORE runtime verification. Use when a failure/warning/anomaly surfaces, a plan rests on a high-risk unproven assumption, a pattern is about to land in a second surface, a sleep/retry/magic-number is tempting, or a cutover commit is about to ship. ([opencharly/marketplace](https://github.com/opencharly/marketplace/tree/HEAD/internals/skills/strict-policy) · 2 copies)
- **strict-property-access**: Enforce strict, schema-verified property access in all generated or edited code. Use this skill whenever writing, modifying, or reviewing code that accesses object properties, dict keys, API response fields, or config values — in any language. Especially triggers when the exact property name or data shape is uncertain, when mapping external data (API responses, JSON, DB rows) to internal types, or when tempted to write fallback chains like `obj.x or obj.X`, `data.get('a') or data.get('A')`, or `obj?.userId ?? obj?.user_id`. Also use when reviewing existing code for these patterns. ([xs2tariqrasheed/strict-property-access](https://github.com/xs2tariqrasheed/strict-property-access/tree/HEAD/plugins/strict-property-access/skills/strict-property-access) · ★1)
- **strict-pydantic-config-with-suggestions**: Forbid unknown fields on every config model and reject misspellings with a "did you mean ..." suggestion drawn from difflib.get_close_matches, while stripping whitespace from all string values automatically. ([kjuhwa/skills-hub](https://github.com/kjuhwa/skills-hub/tree/HEAD/skills/configuration/strict-pydantic-config-with-suggestions))
- **strict-refactor**: Use when a large function, component, or call site needs decomposing into named single-purpose units — extract computation blocks, guard clauses, setup phases, UI subtrees, or bundled parameter groups, or move a function to a better module. Triggers on "strict refactor", "/strict-refactor", "extract function", "extract component", "break up this function", "split into smaller functions", "group these parameters". - **strict-research**: Strict literature research mode for DeepScientist quests: broad candidate scouting first, reliability verification, conservative filtering, quest-local PDF download, then iterative bibliography reading notes before answering/writing. - **strict-review**: This skill should be used when performing code review on a diff (whether implemented by another agent or an external PR), enforcing a strict reviewer stance, applying a mandatory 9-item checklist, demanding evidence, and giving specific改法 instead of vague critique. ([Lee-W/maigo](https://github.com/Lee-W/maigo/tree/HEAD/skills/strict-review) · ★14)
- **strict-review**: 厳格なシニアエンジニアとして敵対的なコードレビューを実施する。セキュリティ・パフォーマンス・テスト・設計の観点で問題を検出し、すべて解決されるまで承認しない。 ([kaito0079/claude-plugins](https://github.com/kaito0079/claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/kaito-review/skills/strict-review))
- **strict-simplify**: Use when reviewing code to replace redundant or verbose logic with a provably-equivalent simpler form — custom code that duplicates a stdlib/builtin or an existing project function, dead/no-op arguments, collapsible redundant expressions, duplicate inline logic that reimplements something already defined in the codebase. Triggers on "strict simplify", "/strict-simplify", "reduce redundant code". Does not restructure, rename, reformat, optimize, or fix bugs. ([uhstray-io/huhhb](https://github.com/uhstray-io/huhhb/tree/HEAD/skills/strict-simplify) · ★5)
- **strict-string-context-verification**: 强制执行字符串字面量重构前必须 100% 追踪完整上下文与上下游数据管线（Upstream & Downstream Data Pipeline）的严苛审计规范。严禁凭空发明新常量，严禁无证据修改。 ([choicenew/yourcallyourrule](https://github.com/choicenew/yourcallyourrule/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/strict-string-context-verification) · ★3)
- **strict-system-qa-protocol**: Run 9 concrete tool-based verifies with evidence when user demands strict QA on a deployed system ('verify nghiêm ngặt', 'check lại', 'QA system', 'đã run được chưa?'). Each verify = 1 tool command (md5, stat, grep, diff, count) with inline evidence. Report format must be numbered, evidence-based, no 'should work' handwaving. Use when user signals: 'verify', 'tự verify', 'QA nghiêm ngặt', 'đảm bảo', 'tự check'. Different from qa-gate (which gates individual steps) — this is for verifying a DEPLOYED SYSTEM end-to-end. ([tuananh4865/hermes-backup](https://github.com/tuananh4865/hermes-backup/tree/HEAD/skills/strict-system-qa-protocol))
- **strict-tdd**: Strict RED-&gt;GREEN-&gt;REFACTOR test-driven development with enforcement. Never write production code before a failing test. Atomic commits per TDD cycle. Use when asking market makers to add book liquidity before a large order, or when building a quoter bot that reacts to RFQs.
